All nine encountered subjects and whole READY sections were reviewed against frozen B0255 views using exact completed B0254 reuse. Ume’s competitive center, Emoco’s abrasive agency, Hina’s capacity to dissent, Haru’s inclusive invitation and Hime’s role-specific investment have local support here. ### `REV-0045` — REVISE: two B0251 collective reference IDs

B0251's historical collective-context prose paired Uoharu with UNIT-0005 and Nonohara Tofu Shop with UNIT-0006. The complete current unit rows and the exact admitted B0251 pages establish the reverse routing: Uoharu/Harumi household is UNIT-0006; Nonohara household/tofu shop is UNIT-0005. This qualification supersedes those two references for current use. Their original bytes, identifiers, B0251 reading and frozen receipts remain preserved. No new household membership, source admission or retrospective character knowledge follows.

- **Old interpretation:** historical Uoharu reference used UNIT-0005, while Nonohara Tofu Shop used UNIT-0006.
- **New interpretation:** route Uoharu/Harumi household to UNIT-0006 and Nonohara household/tofu shop to UNIT-0005.
- **Trigger and counterevidence:** exact already admitted B0251 source pages and the complete current unit rows distinguish sashimi from Uoharu and Hime's Nonohara tofu; this is a reciprocal reference swap, not an affiliation merger.
- **Class:** factual reference-routing repair within the owned series root; no new source admission or analytical-horizon advance for these old pages.
- **Affected records and dependency repair:** the two preserved B0251 collective-context bullets in ENTITY component010; current entity qualification and this reading supply corrected routes. No identity, READY, raw source or historical byte is rewritten.
- **Effective horizon and history:** current B0258/12.167 qualification only, retaining the exact B0251 component and sealed reading.

- Uoharu / existing `UNIT-0006`: Shinju reports a sashimi donation in the party context. This is an attributed material contribution, not a new named supplier, complete expense audit or merger of shop/household and public performance roles. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0208.json__28adabcecdc5dfee?doc=primary&log=59#/Pages/59`.

- Nonohara Tofu Shop / existing `UNIT-0005`: Hime identifies the live-specific premium tofu and later offers it as a return gift; the finale adds tofu/okara provision. Taste and uniqueness comparisons remain attributed, and no independently watched cooking, purchase or financial settlement is supplied. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0205.json__fd335e868ed331ad?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0205.json__fd335e868ed331ad?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12` (complete P7-12 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0206.json__9f071cf7e585168e?doc=primary&log=48#/Pages/48` through `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0206.json__9f071cf7e585168e?doc=primary&log=56#/Pages/56` (complete P48-56 context); `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0206.json__9f071cf7e585168e?doc=primary&log=81#/Pages/81`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0208.json__28adabcecdc5dfee?doc=primary&log=60#/Pages/60`.

### `AV-0205` — complete native text and selected static evidence; voice unheard

B0258 closes the six complete documents of original Event family 200600, 姉妹ガールズバンド！～4Uの新弟子！？～の巻, catalog 933–938, at SourceLock 12.167 after the genuine B0257/12.166 entering state. All 693 native pages comprise 576 written records and 117 command-only pages, with 523 dialogue-voice carriers and 5,952 visual references. Each chapter was completely read and frozen before the next; the whole family was reconstructed after all six. Selected stills were actually reviewed. All 530 named voice resources remain unheard, and runtime remains unobserved.
